======================
FILE: README.md
==============================

# CSDM Deep Linker Sandbox — Phase 3.7

Adds Relationship Management UX: right-click relationship menu, relationship details, change relationship type, reverse direction with validation, delete relationship, and duplicate relationship checks in Model Quality Advisor. Preserves smart routing, persisted positions, View Modes, Reset Layout, and prior behavior.


## Load Saved Model enhancement
- Adds `GET /api/csdm/models`, `GET /api/csdm/model`, and `POST /api/csdm/load`.
- Adds `public/loadModels.js`, which renders an always-visible floating **Load Saved Model** button.
- Loading a backup creates a fresh backup of the current `csdmData.json` before replacing it.



======================
FILE: server.js
==============================

const express = require('express');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { validateGraph } = require('./shared/csdmValidator');

const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
const DATA_FILE = path.join(__dirname, 'csdmData.json');
const BACKUP_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'backups');
const BACKUP_FILE_PATTERN = /^csdmData\..+\.json$/;

app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' }));
app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
app.use('/shared', express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'shared')));

function readJson(filePath, message) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8'));
  } catch (err) {
    err.message = `${message}: ${err.message}`;
    throw err;
  }
}

function readModel() {
  return readJson(DATA_FILE, 'Unable to read CSDM data');
}

function normalizeModel(model) {
  return {
    schemaVersion: model.schemaVersion || 'phase-3.7',
    seedModel: model.seedModel || 'Custom / Edited Model',
    nodes: model.nodes || [],
    edges: (model.edges || []).map(e => ({
      from: e.from,
      to: e.to,
      label: e.label || e.relationship
    }))
  };
}

function validateOrThrow(model) {
  const normalized = normalizeModel(model);
  const v = validateGraph(normalized);
  if (!v.valid) {
    const err = new Error('CSDM model validation failed.');
    err.status = 400;
    err.details = v.errors;
    throw err;
  }
  return normalized;
}

function backupCurrentModel() {
  fs.mkdirSync(BACKUP_DIR, { recursive: true });
  if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_FILE)) return null;

  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  const backup = path.join(BACKUP_DIR, `csdmData.${stamp}.json`);
  fs.copyFileSync(DATA_FILE, backup);
  return backup;
}

function writeModel(model) {
  const normalized = validateOrThrow(model);
  fs.mkdirSync(BACKUP_DIR, { recursive: true });

  const tmp = `${DATA_FILE}.${process.pid}.${Date.now()}.tmp`;
  const payload = JSON.stringify(normalized, null, 2);

  try {
    backupCurrentModel();
    fs.writeFileSync(tmp, payload, 'utf8');
    JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(tmp, 'utf8'));
    fs.renameSync(tmp, DATA_FILE);
  } catch (err) {
    try {
      if (fs.existsSync(tmp)) fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
    } catch (_) {}
    err.message = `Unable to write CSDM data safely: ${err.message}`;
    throw err;
  }
}

function resolveSavedModelPath(file) {
  if (!file || file === 'current') return DATA_FILE;

  const safeName = path.basename(file);
  if (safeName !== file || !BACKUP_FILE_PATTERN.test(safeName)) {
    const err = new Error('Invalid saved model file name.');
    err.status = 400;
    throw err;
  }

  const fullPath = path.join(BACKUP_DIR, safeName);
  if (!fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
    const err = new Error('Saved model was not found.');
    err.status = 404;
    throw err;
  }

  return fullPath;
}

function summarizeModelFile(filePath, file, displayName, isCurrent) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
  const summary = {
    file,
    displayName,
    isCurrent: !!isCurrent,
    modified: stat.mtime.toISOString(),
    size: stat.size
  };

  try {
    const model = normalizeModel(readJson(filePath, `Unable to read ${displayName}`));
    const v = validateGraph(model);
    summary.schemaVersion = model.schemaVersion;
    summary.seedModel = model.seedModel;
    summary.nodeCount = model.nodes.length;
    summary.edgeCount = model.edges.length;
    summary.valid = v.valid;
    summary.errors = v.errors || [];
  } catch (err) {
    summary.valid = false;
    summary.error = err.message;
  }

  return summary;
}

function listSavedModels() {
  const models = [];

  if (fs.existsSync(DATA_FILE)) {
    models.push(summarizeModelFile(DATA_FILE, 'current', 'Current working model', true));
  }

  if (fs.existsSync(BACKUP_DIR)) {
    const backups = fs.readdirSync(BACKUP_DIR)
      .filter(name => BACKUP_FILE_PATTERN.test(name))
      .map(name => summarizeModelFile(path.join(BACKUP_DIR, name), name, name, false))
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.modified) - new Date(a.modified));
    models.push(...backups);
  }

  return models;
}

function readSavedModel(file) {
  return normalizeModel(readJson(resolveSavedModelPath(file), 'Unable to read saved model'));
}

app.get('/api/csdm', (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    res.json(readModel());
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
});

app.post('/api/csdm', (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    writeModel(req.body);
    res.json({ success: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
});

app.get('/api/csdm/models', (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    res.json({ models: listSavedModels() });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
});

app.get('/api/csdm/model', (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    res.json(readSavedModel(req.query.file || 'current'));
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
});

app.post('/api/csdm/load', (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const file = req.body && req.body.file;
    const model = readSavedModel(file || 'current');

    if (file && file !== 'current') {
      writeModel(model);
    }

    res.json({
      success: true,
      loadedFrom: file || 'current',
      model: readModel()
    });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
});

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => res.json({ status: 'ok', phase: '3.7' }));
app.get('*', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html')));

app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
  const status = err.status || 500;
  res.status(status).json({ error: err.message, details: err.details });
});

if (require.main === module) {
  app.listen(PORT, () => console.log(`CSDM Graph-Linking Engine running at http://localhost:${PORT}`));
}

module.exports = {
  app,
  readModel,
  writeModel,
  listSavedModels,
  readSavedModel
};

function showStartupProblem(message, err){
  console.error(message, err || '');
  const s=document.getElementById('status-text');
  if(s)s.textContent=message+(err&&err.message?': '+err.message:'');
}
function renderFallbackSvgGraph(highlight){
  const el=document.getElementById('network-canvas');
  if(!el)return;
  const nodes=(getFilteredGraph?getFilteredGraph():currentModelData).nodes||currentModelData.nodes||[];
  const edges=(getFilteredGraph?getFilteredGraph():currentModelData).edges||currentModelData.edges||[];
  const xs=nodes.map(n=>(n.metadata&&n.metadata.position&&n.metadata.position.x)||0),ys=nodes.map(n=>(n.metadata&&n.metadata.position&&n.metadata.position.y)||0);
  const minX=Math.min(...xs,0)-260,maxX=Math.max(...xs,0)+260,minY=Math.min(...ys,0)-160,maxY=Math.max(...ys,0)+160;
  const nodeById=new Map(nodes.map(n=>[n.id,n]));
  const parts=[`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="${minX} ${minY} ${maxX-minX} ${maxY-minY}" style="width:100%;height:100%;background:#fff">`];
  parts.push('<defs><marker id="arrow" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto" markerUnits="strokeWidth"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L9,3 z" fill="#64748b"/></marker></defs>');
  edges.forEach(e=>{const f=nodeById.get(e.from),t=nodeById.get(e.to);if(!f||!t)return;const a=f.metadata.position,b=t.metadata.position;parts.push(`<line x1="${a.x}" y1="${a.y}" x2="${b.x}" y2="${b.y}" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#arrow)"/>`);parts.push(`<text x="${(a.x+b.x)/2}" y="${(a.y+b.y)/2-6}" font-size="11" fill="#334155" text-anchor="middle">${esc(e.label||e.relationship||'')}</text>`)});
  nodes.forEach(n=>{const p=n.metadata.position,c=(window.CSDM_SCHEMA.visuals.domainMap[n.type]||{color:'#64748b'}).color,w=190,h=54;parts.push(`<rect x="${p.x-w/2}" y="${p.y-h/2}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="8" fill="${c}" stroke="#0f172a"/>`);parts.push(`<text x="${p.x}" y="${p.y-4}" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle">${esc(n.label)}</text>`);parts.push(`<text x="${p.x}" y="${p.y+14}" font-size="10" fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle">[${esc(n.type)}]</text>`)});
  parts.push('</svg>');
  el.innerHTML=parts.join('');
  const s=document.getElementById('status-text');
  if(s)s.textContent='Fallback diagram rendered. Vis Network did not load, so editing interactions are limited.';
}
let currentModelData={nodes:[],edges:[]},networkInstance=null,selectedNodeId=null,selectedEdgeIndex=null,initialFullDemo=null,lastSavedModel=null,undoStack=[],redoStack=[],activeViewMode='full',neighborhoodNodeId=null,layoutDragSnapshot=null;const domainMap=window.CSDM_SCHEMA.visuals.domainMap,GUIDED_INFRA_TARGET_TYPES=['Infrastructure CI','Compute Cluster','Kubernetes Cluster','Virtualization Cluster','Database Instance'],layerTypeMap={layerBusinessDesign:['Business Capability','Business Application'],layerApplicationService:['Application Service'],layerServiceConsumption:['Business Service Offering'],layerCompute:['Infrastructure CI','Compute Cluster','Kubernetes Cluster','Virtualization Cluster','Compute Node'],layerRuntime:['Namespace','Workload','Pod','Container','Container Image'],layerData:['Database Instance'],layerStorage:['Storage Volume'],layerNetwork:['Load Balancer','Ingress','Network Segment','Subnet','DNS Record'],layerSecurity:['Certificate','Key Vault','Secret']};async function loadSandbox(){try{const response=await fetch('/api/csdm');if(!response.ok)throw new Error('/api/csdm returned '+response.status);currentModelData=await response.json()}catch(err){showStartupProblem('Could not load /api/csdm; using embedded demo data',err);currentModelData=clone(window.CSDM_FALLBACK_DATA||{nodes:[],edges:[]})}try{if(window.CSDM_MIGRATIONS)currentModelData=window.CSDM_MIGRATIONS.migrateModel(currentModelData,window.CSDM_SCHEMA)}catch(err){console.warn('Migration skipped',err)}ensurePositions();lastSavedModel=clone(currentModelData);initialFullDemo=clone(currentModelData);renderGraph();bindMenus();updateStatusBar()}function clone(x){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x))}function getNode(id){return currentModelData.nodes.find(n=>n.id===id)}function getEdge(i){return currentModelData.edges[i]}function isInfraType(t){return window.CSDM_SCHEMA.infrastructureTypes().includes(t)}function defaultPositionFor(n){const lvl=(domainMap[n.type]||{level:5}).level,same=currentModelData.nodes.filter(x=>(domainMap[x.type]||{level:5}).level===lvl),i=same.findIndex(x=>x.id===n.id);return{x:Math.round((i-(same.length-1)/2)*260),y:lvl*150-450}}function applyDefaultLayout(){currentModelData.nodes.forEach(n=>{n.metadata=n.metadata||{};n.metadata.position=defaultPositionFor(n)})}function ensurePositions(){currentModelData.nodes.forEach(n=>{n.metadata=n.metadata||{};if(!n.metadata.position)n.metadata.position=defaultPositionFor(n)})}function relationshipSentence(e){const f=getNode(e.from),t=getNode(e.to),l=e.label||e.relationship;return f&&t?`${f.label} ${l.toLowerCase()} ${t.label}`:l}function relationshipExplanation(e){const f=getNode(e.from),t=getNode(e.to),l=e.label||e.relationship;if(!f||!t)return'This relationship references a missing node.';const r=window.CSDM_SCHEMA.getAllowedRelationship(f.type,t.type,l);return r?`${f.label} [${f.type}] --${l}--> ${t.label} [${t.type}]\n\nMeaning: ${relationshipSentence(e)}.\n\nWhy valid: ${r.explanation}`:`Invalid relationship: ${f.type} --${l}--> ${t.type}.`}function validLabelsForNodes(fromId,toId){const f=getNode(fromId),t=getNode(toId);return f&&t?window.CSDM_SCHEMA.getValidRelationshipLabels(f.type,t.type):[]}function approxNodeBox(n){const p=n.metadata.position,w=Math.max(160,Math.min(330,(n.label.length+n.type.length)*6+55)),h=58;return{left:p.x-w/2,right:p.x+w/2,top:p.y-h/2,bottom:p.y+h/2}}function ccw(A,B,C){return(C.y-A.y)*(B.x-A.x)>(B.y-A.y)*(C.x-A.x)}function segIntersects(a,b,c,d){return ccw(a,c,d)!==ccw(b,c,d)&&ccw(a,b,c)!==ccw(a,b,d)}function lineIntersectsBox(a,b,box){if((a.x<box.left&&b.x<box.left)||(a.x>box.right&&b.x>box.right)||(a.y<box.top&&b.y<box.top)||(a.y>box.bottom&&b.y>box.bottom))return false;if(a.x>box.left&&a.x<box.right&&a.y>box.top&&a.y<box.bottom)return true;if(b.x>box.left&&b.x<box.right&&b.y>box.top&&b.y<box.bottom)return true;const tl={x:box.left,y:box.top},tr={x:box.right,y:box.top},br={x:box.right,y:box.bottom},bl={x:box.left,y:box.bottom};return segIntersects(a,b,tl,tr)||segIntersects(a,b,tr,br)||segIntersects(a,b,br,bl)||segIntersects(a,b,bl,tl)}function edgeNeedsCurve(e,nodes){const from=getNode(e.from),to=getNode(e.to);if(!from||!to)return false;const a=from.metadata.position,b=to.metadata.position;return nodes.some(n=>n.id!==e.from&&n.id!==e.to&&lineIntersectsBox(a,b,approxNodeBox(n)))}function edgeSmoothFor(e,nodes){return edgeNeedsCurve(e,nodes)?{enabled:true,type:'curvedCW',roundness:.22}:false}function getFilteredGraph(){let visible=new Set(currentModelData.nodes.map(n=>n.id));if(activeViewMode==='business')visible=new Set(currentModelData.nodes.filter(n=>['Business Capability','Business Application','Application Service','Business Service Offering'].includes(n.type)).map(n=>n.id));else if(activeViewMode==='infraOnly')visible=new Set(currentModelData.nodes.filter(n=>isInfraType(n.type)).map(n=>n.id));else if(activeViewMode==='serviceInfra')visible=new Set(currentModelData.nodes.filter(n=>['Business Application','Application Service','Business Service Offering'].includes(n.type)||isInfraType(n.type)).map(n=>n.id));else if(activeViewMode==='neighborhood'&&neighborhoodNodeId){visible=new Set([neighborhoodNodeId]);currentModelData.edges.forEach(e=>{if(e.from===neighborhoodNodeId)visible.add(e.to);if(e.to===neighborhoodNodeId)visible.add(e.from)})}else if(layerTypeMap[activeViewMode])visible=new Set(currentModelData.nodes.filter(n=>layerTypeMap[activeViewMode].includes(n.type)).map(n=>n.id));return{nodes:currentModelData.nodes.filter(n=>visible.has(n.id)),edges:currentModelData.edges.map((e,i)=>({...e,_index:i})).filter(e=>visible.has(e.from)&&visible.has(e.to))}}function markChange(d,fn){const before=clone(currentModelData);const result=fn();if(result===false){currentModelData=before;ensurePositions();return false}if(JSON.stringify(before)===JSON.stringify(currentModelData)){currentModelData=before;return false}currentModelData.schemaVersion='phase-3.7';currentModelData.seedModel='Custom / Edited Model';undoStack.push({description:d,before,after:clone(currentModelData)});redoStack=[];renderGraph();updateStatusBar();return true}function undoChange(){if(!undoStack.length)return;const i=undoStack.pop();redoStack.push({description:i.description,before:clone(currentModelData),after:i.after});currentModelData=clone(i.before);ensurePositions();renderGraph();updateStatusBar()}function redoChange(){if(!redoStack.length)return;const i=redoStack.pop();undoStack.push({description:i.description,before:clone(currentModelData),after:i.after});currentModelData=clone(i.after);ensurePositions();renderGraph();updateStatusBar()}async function persistChanges(){const v=window.CSDM_VALIDATOR.validateGraph(currentModelData);if(!v.valid)return alert(v.errors.join('\n'));if((await fetch('/api/csdm',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(currentModelData)})).ok){lastSavedModel=clone(currentModelData);undoStack=[];redoStack=[];updateStatusBar();alert('Model saved.')}}function hasUnsavedChanges(){return JSON.stringify(currentModelData)!==JSON.stringify(lastSavedModel)}function discardChanges(){if(!hasUnsavedChanges())return;if(confirm('Discard all unsaved changes and return to the last saved model?')){currentModelData=clone(lastSavedModel);ensurePositions();undoStack=[];redoStack=[];renderGraph();updateStatusBar();closeDialog();hideMenus()}}function setViewMode(m){if(m==='neighborhood'&&!neighborhoodNodeId){if(selectedNodeId)neighborhoodNodeId=selectedNodeId;else{alert('Select or right-click a node first to use Selected Node Neighborhood view.');document.getElementById('view-mode').value=activeViewMode;return}}activeViewMode=m;document.getElementById('view-mode').value=m;hideMenus();renderGraph();updateStatusBar()}function clearViewFilter(){activeViewMode='full';neighborhoodNodeId=null;document.getElementById('view-mode').value='full';renderGraph();updateStatusBar()}function showNeighborhood(id){selectedNodeId=id;neighborhoodNodeId=id;setViewMode('neighborhood')}function viewLabel(m){return({full:'Full Model',business:'Business View',serviceInfra:'Service-to-Infrastructure View',infraOnly:'Infrastructure Only View',neighborhood:'Selected Node Neighborhood',layerBusinessDesign:'Blue — Business / Design',layerApplicationService:'Green — Application Service',layerServiceConsumption:'Amber — Service Consumption',layerCompute:'Gray — Compute Infrastructure',layerRuntime:'Teal — Runtime / Containers',layerData:'Purple — Data',layerStorage:'Brown — Storage',layerNetwork:'Cyan — Network',layerSecurity:'Red — Security'})[m]||m}function updateStatusBar(){const c=domainMap[n.type]||{color:'#999',level:5},h=highlight?.nodes?.includes(n.id),p=n.metadata.position;return{id:n.id,label:`${n.label}\n[${n.type}]`,x:p.x,y:p.y,level:c.level,color:{background:h?'#facc15':c.color,border:h?'#991b1b':'#0f172a'},borderWidth:h?4:1,font:{color:h?'#0f172a':'#fff',size:11,bold:true},shape:'box',margin:{top:10,bottom:10,left:14,right:14}}}),es=fg.edges.map(e=>({id:e._index,from:e.from,to:e.to,label:e.label||e.relationship,title:relationshipExplanation(e),arrows:'to',smooth:edgeSmoothFor(e,fg.nodes),color:{color:highlight?.edges?.includes(e._index)?'#f59e0b':'#94a3b8'},width:highlight?.edges?.includes(e._index)?4:1,font:{align:'top',size:9}}));if(networkInstance)networkInstance.destroy();networkInstance=new vis.DataSet(es)},{interaction:{dragNodes:true,dragView:true,selectable:true,hover:true},layout:{hierarchical:false,improvedLayout:false},physics:{enabled:false},edges:{smooth:false}});networkInstance.on('click',p=>{hideMenus();if(p.nodes?.length)selectedNodeId=p.nodes[0]});networkInstance.on('dragStart',p=>{if(p.nodes?.length)layoutDragSnapshot=clone(currentModelData)});networkInstance.on('dragEnd',p=>{if(!p.nodes?.length||!layoutDragSnapshot)return;const Model';undoStack.push({description:'Move node r=pane.getBoundingClientRect(),x=e.clientX-r.left,y=e.clientY-r.top;if(e.button===2){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();hideMenus();const [${n.type}]`;positionMenu('node-menu',x,y)}else positionMenu('canvas-menu',x,y);return false}if(e.button===0&&!cm.contains(e.target)&&!nm.contains(e.target)&&!em.contains(e.target))hideMenus()},{capture:true});pane.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();return (function () {
  const BUTTON_ID = 'csdm-load-saved-model-button';
  const MODAL_ID = 'csdm-load-saved-model-modal';

  function esc(value) {
    return String(value ?? '')
      .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
      .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
      .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
      .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
      .replaceAll("'", '&#039;');
  }

  function prettyDate(value) {
    try {
      return new Date(value).toLocaleString();
    } catch (_) {
      return value || '';
    }
  }

  function ensureStyles() {
    if (document.getElementById('csdm-load-saved-model-styles')) return;

    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = 'csdm-load-saved-model-styles';
    style.textContent = `
      #${BUTTON_ID} {
        position: fixed;
        top: 76px;
        right: 22px;
        z-index: 100000;
        border: none;
        border-radius: 10px;
        padding: 10px 14px;
        background: #0f766e;
        color: #fff;
        font: 700 13px Segoe UI, Tahoma, sans-serif;
        box-shadow: 0 14px 30px rgba(15,23,42,.28);
        cursor: pointer;
      }
      #${BUTTON_ID}:hover { background: #115e59; }
      #${MODAL_ID} {
        position: fixed;
        inset: 0;
        z-index: 100001;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        background: rgba(15,23,42,.42);
        color: #0f172a;
        font-family: Segoe UI, Tahoma, sans-serif;
      }
      #${MODAL_ID}.hidden { display: none !important; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-card {
        width: min(780px, calc(100vw - 36px));
        max-height: calc(100vh - 36px);
        overflow: auto;
        border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
        border-radius: 14px;
        background: #fff;
        box-shadow: 0 25px 70px rgba(15,23,42,.35);
      }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: space-between;
        padding: 14px 16px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
      }
      #${MODAL_ID} h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 16px; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-body { padding: 16px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
      #${MODAL_ID} label { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; color: #64748b; text-transform: uppercase; }
      #${MODAL_ID} select { width: 100%; padding: 8px; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 12px; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-box { border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 10px; padding: 10px; background: #f8fafc; line-height: 1.55; font-size: 12px; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-good { background: #f0fdf4; border-color: #bbf7d0; color: #166534; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-bad { background: #fef2f2; border-color: #fecaca; color: #991b1b; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .lm-actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 4px; }
      #${MODAL_ID} button { padding: 8px 10px; border: none; border-radius: 7px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer; background: #2563eb; color: white; }
      #${MODAL_ID} button.secondary { background: #64748b; }
      #${MODAL_ID} .muted { color: #64748b; font-size: 12px; }
    `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }

  function ensureButton() {
    ensureStyles();
    if (document.getElementById(BUTTON_ID)) return;

    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.id = BUTTON_ID;
    button.type = 'button';
    button.textContent = 'Load Saved Model';
    button.addEventListener('click', openLoadDialog);
    document.body.appendChild(button);
  }

  function ensureModal() {
    ensureStyles();
    let modal = document.getElementById(MODAL_ID);
    if (modal) return modal;

    modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = MODAL_ID;
    modal.className = 'hidden';
    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="lm-card">
        <div class="lm-header">
          <h2>Load Saved Model</h2>
          <button class="secondary" id="lm-close" type="button">Close</button>
        </div>
        <div class="lm-body" id="lm-body"></div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
    modal.querySelector('#lm-close').addEventListener('click', closeLoadDialog);
    modal.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === modal) closeLoadDialog(); });
    return modal;
  }

  function closeLoadDialog() {
    const modal = document.getElementById(MODAL_ID);
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
  }

  async function openLoadDialog() {
    const modal = ensureModal();
    const body = modal.querySelector('#lm-body');
    modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="lm-box">Loading saved models...</div>';

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/csdm/models');
      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(payload.error || 'The server did not return the saved model list.');
      renderModels(payload.models || []);
    } catch (err) {
      body.innerHTML = `<div class="lm-box lm-bad"><strong>Could not load saved models.</strong><br>${esc(err.message)}<br><br>Make sure you restarted the Node server after extracting this version.</div>`;
    }
  }

  function renderModels(models) {
    const modal = ensureModal();
    const body = modal.querySelector('#lm-body');

    if (!models.length) {
      body.innerHTML = '<div class="lm-box lm-bad">No saved models were found.</div>';
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = `
      <div class="muted">Select the current model or a timestamped backup from the backups folder. Loading a backup first creates a new backup of the current model, then replaces the current model.</div>
      <div>
        <label>Saved model</label>
        <select id="lm-select">
          ${models.map(m => `<option value="${esc(m.file)}">${esc(m.displayName)} — ${Number(m.nodeCount || 0)} nodes / ${Number(m.edgeCount || 0)} relationships — ${m.valid ? 'valid' : 'invalid'}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div id="lm-detail"></div>
      <div class="lm-actions">
        <button class="secondary" type="button" id="lm-refresh">Refresh</button>
        <button class="secondary" type="button" id="lm-cancel">Cancel</button>
        <button type="button" id="lm-load">Load Selected Model</button>
      </div>`;

    const select = body.querySelector('#lm-select');

    function updateDetail() {
      const m = models.find(x => x.file === select.value);
      const detail = body.querySelector('#lm-detail');
      if (!m) {
        detail.innerHTML = '';
        return;
      }

      detail.innerHTML = `<div class="lm-box ${m.valid ? 'lm-good' : 'lm-bad'}">
        <strong>${esc(m.displayName)}</strong><br>
        Schema: ${esc(m.schemaVersion || 'Unknown')}<br>
        Seed Model: ${esc(m.seedModel || 'Unknown')}<br>
        Nodes: ${Number(m.nodeCount || 0)}<br>
        Relationships: ${Number(m.edgeCount || 0)}<br>
        Last Modified: ${esc(prettyDate(m.modified))}<br>
        Status: ${m.valid ? 'Valid' : 'Invalid'}${m.error ? `<br>Error: ${esc(m.error)}` : ''}
      </div>`;
    }

    select.addEventListener('change', updateDetail);
    body.querySelector('#lm-refresh').addEventListener('click', openLoadDialog);
    body.querySelector('#lm-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeLoadDialog);
    body.querySelector('#lm-load').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const selected = select.value;
      const m = models.find(x => x.file === selected);
      if (!m) return alert('Select a saved model first.');
      if (!m.valid) return alert('This saved model is invalid and cannot be loaded.');

      const message = m.isCurrent
        ? 'Reload the current working model from disk?'
        : 'Load this backup as the current working model? The current model will be backed up first.';
      if (!confirm(message)) return;

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/csdm/load', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ file: selected })
        });
        const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(payload.error || 'Unable to load selected model.');
        alert('Saved model loaded. The page will refresh.');
        window.location.reload();
      } catch (err) {
        alert(err.message);
      }
    });

    updateDetail();
  }

  window.CSDM_OPEN_LOAD_MODELS = openLoadDialog;

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ensureButton);
  } else {
    ensureButton();
  }
})();

FILE: shared\csdmValidator.js
==============================

(function(root,factory){if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=factory(require('./csdmSchema'));else root.CSDM_VALIDATOR=factory(root.CSDM_SCHEMA);})(typeof self!=='undefined'?self:this,function(schema){function validateGraph(model){const errors=[],byId=new Map();for(const n of model.nodes||[]){if(!schema.nodeTypes[n.type])errors.push(`Node ${n.id} has unsupported type: ${n.type}.`);byId.set(n.id,n)}for(const e of model.edges||[]){const f=byId.get(e.from),t=byId.get(e.to),l=e.label||e.relationship;if(!f||!t)errors.push('Edge references missing endpoint.');else if(!schema.getAllowedRelationship(f.type,t.type,l))errors.push(`Invalid relationship: ${f.type} --${l}--> ${t.type}.`)}return{valid:errors.length===0,errors,warnings:[]}}return{validateGraph,validateRelationship:(f,t,l)=>({valid:!!schema.getAllowedRelationship(f?.type,t?.type,l),message:schema.getAllowedRelationship(f?.type,t?.type,l)?.explanation||'Invalid relationship'})};});



======================
FILE: shared\graphService.js
==============================

(function(root,factory){if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=factory();else root.CSDM_GRAPH=factory();})(typeof self!=='undefined'?self:this,function(){function getNode(model,id){return model&&Array.isArray(model.nodes)?model.nodes.find(n=>n.id===id):null}function edgeLabel(e){return e&&(e.label||e.relationship)}function isDuplicateEdge(model,from,to,label,ignoreIndex){return !!(model&&Array.isArray(model.edges)&&model.edges.some((e,i)=>i!==ignoreIndex&&e.from===from&&e.to===to&&edgeLabel(e)===label))}function validateEdge(model,schema,from,to,label,options){const opts=options||{},errors=[],f=getNode(model,from),t=getNode(model,to);if(!from)errors.push('From node is required.');if(!to)errors.push('To node is required.');if(!label)errors.push('Relationship label is required.');if(from&&!f)errors.push(`From node not found: ${from}.`);if(to&&!t)errors.push(`To node not found: ${to}.`);if(f&&t&&schema&&typeof schema.getAllowedRelationship==='function'&&!schema.getAllowedRelationship(f.type,t.type,label))errors.push(`Invalid relationship: ${f.type} --${label}--> ${t.type}.`);if(!opts.allowDuplicate&&isDuplicateEdge(model,from,to,label,opts.ignoreIndex))errors.push(`Duplicate relationship: ${from} --${label}--> ${to}.`);return{valid:errors.length===0,errors}}function addEdge(model,schema,edge,options){const label=edgeLabel(edge),v=validateEdge(model,schema,edge.from,edge.to,label,options);if(!v.valid)return{added:false,errors:v.errors};model.edges.push({from:edge.from,to:edge.to,label});return{added:true,errors:[],edge:model.edges[model.edges.length-1]}}function reverseRelationship(model,schema,index){const e=model&&model.edges&&model.edges[index];if(!e)return{reversed:false,errors:[`Relationship ${index} was not found.`]};const label=edgeLabel(e),v=validateEdge(model,schema,e.to,e.from,label,{ignoreIndex:index});if(!v.valid)return{reversed:false,errors:v.errors};const old=e.from;e.from=e.to;e.to=old;e.label=label;delete e.relationship;return{reversed:true,errors:[],edge:e}}return{getNode,edgeLabel,isDuplicateEdge,validateEdge,addEdge,reverseRelationship};});

FILE: shared\migrations.js
==============================

(function(root,factory){if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=factory();else root.CSDM_MIGRATIONS=factory();})(typeof self!=='undefined'?self:this,function(){const CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION='phase-3.7';function migrateModel(model,schema){const m=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(model||{}));m.nodes=Array.isArray(m.nodes)?m.nodes:[];m.edges=Array.isArray(m.edges)?m.edges:[];m.nodes.forEach(n=>{n.metadata=n.metadata||{};if(!n.domain&&schema&&schema.nodeTypes&&schema.nodeTypes[n.type])n.domain=schema.nodeTypes[n.type].domain;if(!n.metadata.position){const info=schema&&schema.nodeTypes&&schema.nodeTypes[n.type]?schema.nodeTypes[n.type]:{level:5};n.metadata.position={x:0,y:(info.level||5)*150-450}}});m.edges.forEach(e=>{if(!e.label&&e.relationship)e.label=e.relationship;delete e.relationship});m.schemaVersion=CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION;return m}return{CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,migrateModel};});



======================
FILE: shared\modelState.js
==============================

(function(root,factory){if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=factory();else root.CSDM_STATE=factory();})(typeof self!=='undefined'?self:this,function(){function clone(v){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}function createState(initialModel){let current=clone(initialModel),saved=clone(initialModel);const undoStack=[],redoStack=[];function currentModel(){return current}function hasUnsavedChanges(){return JSON.stringify(current)!==JSON.stringify(saved)}function apply(description,mutator){const before=clone(current),result=mutator(current);if(result===false||JSON.stringify(before)===JSON.stringify(current)){current=before;return false}undoStack.push({description,before,after:clone(current)});redoStack.length=0;return true}function undo(){if(!undoStack.length)return false;const item=undoStack.pop();redoStack.push({description:item.description,before:clone(current),after:item.after});current=clone(item.before);return true}function redo(){if(!redoStack.length)return false;const item=redoStack.pop();undoStack.push({description:item.description,before:clone(current),after:item.after});current=clone(item.after);return true}function save(){saved=clone(current);undoStack.length=0;redoStack.length=0}function discard(){current=clone(saved);undoStack.length=0;redoStack.length=0}return{currentModel,hasUnsavedChanges,apply,undo,redo,save,discard,undoStack,redoStack}}return{clone,createState};});

FILE: tests\run-tests.js

const assert=require('assert');const schema=require('../shared/csdmSchema');const{validateGraph}=require('../shared/csdmValidator');const graph=require('../shared/graphService');const state=require('../shared/modelState');const{migrateModel,CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}=require('../shared/migrations');function fixture(){return{schemaVersion:CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,seedModel:'Test',nodes:[{id:'cap',label:'Capability',type:'Business Capability',domain:'Design & Planning',metadata:{}},{id:'app',label:'Application',type:'Business Application',domain:'Design & Planning',metadata:{}},{id:'svc',label:'Service',type:'Application Service',domain:'Service Delivery',metadata:{}},{id:'cluster',label:'Cluster',type:'Kubernetes Cluster',domain:'Infrastructure / Compute',metadata:{}}],edges:[]}}function testValidatorRules(){const m=fixture();m.edges.push({from:'app',to:'cap',label:'Provides'});assert.strictEqual(validateGraph(m).valid,true);m.edges.push({from:'cap',to:'app',label:'Provides'});assert.strictEqual(validateGraph(m).valid,false)}function testEdgeCreationAndDuplicateDetection(){const m=fixture();assert.strictEqual(graph.addEdge(m,schema,{from:'app',to:'cap',label:'Provides'}).added,true);const dup=graph.addEdge(m,schema,{from:'app',to:'cap',label:'Provides'});assert.strictEqual(dup.added,false);assert.match(dup.errors.join('\n'),/Duplicate/)}function testReverseRelationshipBehavior(){const m=fixture();graph.addEdge(m,schema,{from:'svc',to:'cluster',label:'Runs on'});assert.strictEqual(graph.reverseRelationship(m,schema,0).reversed,false)}function testSaveDiscardUndoRedo(){const s=state.createState(fixture());s.apply('add',m=>graph.addEdge(m,schema,{from:'app',to:'cap',label:'Provides'}).added);assert.strictEqual(s.currentModel().edges.length,1);assert.strictEqual(s.undo(),true);assert.strictEqual(s.currentModel().edges.length,0);assert.strictEqual(s.redo(),true);s.save();s.apply('add2',m=>graph.addEdge(m,schema,{from:'svc',to:'app',label:'Instantiates'}).added);assert.strictEqual(s.currentModel().edges.length,2);s.discard();assert.strictEqual(s.currentModel().edges.length,1)}function testMigration(){const m=fixture();m.schemaVersion='phase-3.5.2';m.nodes.forEach(n=>delete n.metadata.position);m.edges.push({from:'app',to:'cap',relationship:'Provides'});const out=migrateModel(m,schema);assert.strictEqual(out.schemaVersion,CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION);assert.ok(out.nodes.every(n=>n.metadata.position));assert.strictEqual(out.edges[0].label,'Provides')}[testValidatorRules,testEdgeCreationAndDuplicateDetection,testReverseRelationshipBehavior,testSaveDiscardUndoRedo,testMigration].forEach(t=>{t();console.log('✓ '+t.name)});console.log('5 tests passed.');
